## Review

**6 out of 10, Needs substantial work.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The route is clear: slides 4 and 5 state the aim, 7 to 12 build the business case, and 13 to 15 close on the plan, and every content slide has a full-sentence takeaway banner. But the execution is uneven. Slides 3, 5, 11, 12, 13 and 15 are dense text, tables and tiny diagrams that cannot be read in a briefing, and slide 12's synergy table and slide 15's table are close to unreadable. The charts on slides 9, 10 and 14 are readable, though slide 9 repeats the same chart three times with small labels clipped inside bars, and slide 14's waterfall has no running totals or connectors, so the core can be recovered but needs substantial revision.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent header with slide number, the green takeaway banner on every content slide, and the simple three-bar comparison on slide 9.

**Most useful repair:** Rebuild slides 5, 12, 13 and 15 so each carries one message with larger type and a simpler diagram or table, and fix the clipped labels in the slide 9 bars and the waterfall on slide 14.

**What would change the score:** I would cut slide 3 and the repeated contents pages 2 and 6 to a single agenda, and break the dense slides into fewer-point pages with the key number as the visual focus.

_Confidence high. 17 of 17 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a cover and contents page that divide the talk between the group's portfolio transformation, led by the CEO, and a business strategy section for Basic & Green Materials, presented by a senior executive. It then outlines the aims of a planned split and the strategic position of a polyolefins integration with Sumitomo Chemical and Idemitsu Kosan, using a one-page map of plant closures, restructuring steps and partner collaborations. The middle section follows the business case in order: domestic polyolefin demand against capacity, the history of consolidation, the earnings trend of the existing joint venture, the ownership and business outline of the deal, integration synergies, green feedstock sourcing, and the profitability baseline. A business plan section links these to a 2030 operating income target and a cumulative cash flow bridge. The deck closes with a short vision statement and a standard forward-looking statements disclaimer. Several slides merit close study. The demand and capacity slide uses simple bar comparisons to show the excess-capacity argument at a glance.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the Mitsui Chemicals Basic & Green Materials business strategy deck have?**

The deck has 17 slides. Slide 1 is the cover and slide 17 carries the forward-looking statements disclaimer and closing messages.

**What does the Mitsui Chemicals deck say about the 2030 operating income target for Basic & Green Materials?**

Slide 15 states that B&GM business will achieve 36 billions of yen of operating income before special items in 2030 by promoting the second phase of restructuring and bolstering downstream businesses. The same slide lists a 2030 ROIC of around 6.5%.

**What does the Mitsui Chemicals deck say about domestic polyolefin demand and capacity?**

Slide 9 says that due to declining domestic demand caused by population decrease and changes in lifestyle, demand for domestically produced polyolefins is expected to shrink further. It also says that with domestic production capacity expected to be halved, business restructuring is inevitable.

**What integration synergies does the Mitsui Chemicals deck expect from the polyolefins integration?**

Slide 12 says the company will generate integration synergies worth more than 8 billion yen through initiatives including consolidation of facilities and product lines. The same slide also notes a goal to improve ROIC in the polyolefins business by 1-2% compared to the current level.

**Layout**

Single full-width slide with a thin white header band across the top holding a blue number badge at left, the title at left and the Mitsui Chemicals logo with a dark grey date box at top right. Below, two horizontal bands stacked: an upper light blue band (Specialty chemicals domains) and a lower light olive band (Green Sustainable Chemicals Division), separated by a grey dotted line. Each band has a left label column with icon tabs, a central chevron-shaped content box, and a dashed callout; a rounded-corner statement box sits at the right of each band. Two grey chevron headers (VISION 2030 and ~2030) run across the top above the bands. No footer; the page number 3 is in the top-left badge.

**Visuals**

Icons and logos only, no photographs or charts. (1) Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right: a blue swoosh mark above or beside the grey wordmark 'Mitsui Chemicals'. (2) L&HC icon: a white line icon of a heart containing a person figure with a curved arrow below, on a dusty pink tile. (3) Mobility icon: a white line icon of a vehicle seat or car interior with a person and sound waves, on a teal-blue tile. (4) ICT icon: a white line icon of a rounded square device with a node and connected dots, on a golden tile. (5) B&GM icon: a white line icon of a factory with a circular arrow around it, on a light olive tile. All icons are white line art, no outlines, no shadows.

**Design**

Background white with a light blue band (#DEEBF7) across the upper middle and a light olive band across the lower part. Title text black #000000, MeiryoUI-Bold sans-serif, about 4.1% of slide height. Body text black #000000 MeiryoUI regular sans-serif about 2.2% of slide height; key phrases in bold red #C00000. Accent colours: navy #072884 badge, blue #2E75B6 vertical tab and rounded box border, olive #6B7B2E border of right green box, green #548235 outline of green chevron, grey #A6A6A6 chevron headers, pale yellow #FFF2CC callouts with dashed borders. Chevron boxes have 1.5 to 2 pt black or green outlines with no fill shadow. Rounded boxes have thick (about 1 pt equivalent) coloured borders and rounded corners of about 12% of box height. Dashed callout borders are black on the upper callout and blue on the lower callout. A light grey dotted horizontal line separates the two bands. Flat design, no gradients or shadows except a faint shadow under the header band.

### Slide 4

**Review notes**

- Review: The route is clear: slides 4 and 5 state the aim, 7 to 12 build the business case, and 13 to 15 close on the plan, and every content slide has a full-sentence takeaway banner.

**Text**

4 / Ideal Vision for the B&GM Business / Aiming to build a globally competitive, Japan-leading business entity / through transformation and collaborations with other companies / Sep. 10, 2025 / Aims of the split / Create in the petrochemical sector a Japan-leading green chemical business entity with a competitive edge against imports, through accelerated transformation and collaborations with other companies / Enable the specialty chemicals domains and the B&GM business – each requiring a different speed of decision-making – to execute strategies under their own governance structures / Support Japanese industry through contributions to economic security, enhanced quality of life via further performance improvements to materials, carbon neutrality, and other initiatives / While we will strive to realize this ideal vision as early as possible, the business will be split off by around 2027. / Ideal vision / L&HC / Mobility / ICT / B&GM / A strong basic & green materials company that supports Japanese industry / Competitors, etc. / Transformation & collaborations with other companies

**Layout**

Single-column header band across the top with a navy numbered tab at the far left, the title beside it, and the Mitsui Chemicals logo and date box at top right; a dark slate subtitle banner spans the full width below the header. Below it, two panels sit side by side on a white ground: a left column with a green-headed box titled Aims of the split, a green arrow pointing right, and a right grey panel titled Ideal vision. Text is left-aligned in the left box and centred in the right panel; the margins are about 1 percent at left and right. No footer or slide-number element other than the navy tab number 4 at top left.

**Visuals**

Diagram on the right: a rounded white box outlined in dark blue (#223F99) containing the Mitsui Chemicals logo (swoosh symbol and wordmark, dark grey wordmark) at its top centre, and four labelled boxes in a 2 by 2 arrangement: L&HC (pink #DCB1B1, white bold text) top left, Mobility (steel blue #679BB0, white bold text) top right, ICT (gold #D8A73A, white bold text) bottom left, and B&GM as a dashed green (#ABB867) outlined box with no fill and grey (#767171) bold text at bottom right. A solid grey (#7F7F7F) down-arrow runs from the blue-outlined box to a large green (#ABB867) rounded rectangle with a red dotted border (#C00000) labelled A strong basic & green materials company that supports Japanese industry. A pink-red curved arrow runs from the B&GM label down to the green rectangle's right side. Below, a grey up-arrow points from a white rounded box with a grey (#7F7F7F) border and grey text Competitors, etc. into the green rectangle, with red text Transformation & collaborations with other companies beside it. The left box has no visual besides text and the green header bar; the green right-pointing chevron arrow sits between the two panels.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Review: The route is clear: slides 4 and 5 state the aim, 7 to 12 build the business case, and 13 to 15 close on the plan, and every content slide has a full-sentence takeaway banner.
- Review: Slides 3, 5, 11, 12, 13 and 15 are dense text, tables and tiny diagrams that cannot be read in a briefing, and slide 12's synergy table and slide 15's table are close to unreadable.
- Suggested repair: Rebuild slides 5, 12, 13 and 15 so each carries one message with larger type and a simpler diagram or table, and fix the clipped labels in the slide 9 bars and the waterfall on slide 14.

**Text**

5 / The strategic positioning of the integration of Polyolefins business / Sep. 10, 2025 / Take the first step toward strengthening Polyolefins business for a strong company that supports Japanese industry / Competitors, etc. / Substantial progress made with internally driven restructuring / Second phase of restructuring (FY25～） / PH Japan / Closure of Ichihara plant end of Oct. FY25 / Stable supply network through collaboration with other companies / PH Overseas / Transfer of Equity Method in China / (Withdrawal from all the global sites ) / PO Japan / High-performance PP plant starts up FY25 / 1PP line at Chiba to close FY26 / Business integration （Sumitomo Chemical ・ Idemitsu Kosan ・ Mitsui Chemicals）FY26★ / MDI Overseas / Study into next production increase for debottlenecking / TDI Japan / Plant downsizing （120Kt ⇒ 50Kt July. FY25） ★ / Crackers / Study into consolidation at Chiba LLP(FY27) / Establish a LLP for the collaboration among three companies in west Japan Aug. FY25 ★ / Accelerate the study into optimal production / Further Transformation & / collaborations with other companies / Highly capital-efficient / PH chain / Highly capital- / efficient strong / polyolefins business / High-profitability / Polyurethane / business centered / on high-performance / MDI / Competitive Green / Crackers / A strong / basic & green / materials / company / that supports / Japanese / industry / Becoming a strong / business entity / able to support / domestic industry

**Layout**

Slide with a navy number tab and title across the top, the Mitsui Chemicals logo top right with a dark grey date box beneath it, and a full-width slate band carrying a yellow subtitle. Below, three columns: a left table-like panel with a green rounded border and row labels (PH, PO, MDI, TDI, Crackers) at left and red or blue text beside them, a centre flow column of four stacked rounded boxes over a large blue right-pointing arrow, and a right tall green rounded panel with a red dotted border. Margins are about 1% left and right; there is no footer and no slide-number text other than the number 5 in the top-left tab.

**Visuals**

Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right: a blue-grey wave or swoosh mark with the wordmark Mitsui Chemicals in dark grey. A large right-pointing block arrow in blue #5B9BD5 with its shaft spanning about x 53 to 72 percent and its triangular tip at about x 82 percent, running from about y 35 to y 97 percent, sitting behind the four centre boxes. A small grey double-headed vertical arrow (#7F7F7F) between the Competitors box and the Further Transformation text at about x 76 percent, y 27 to 36 percent. Red five-point stars (#C00000 or #CF5C5C) beside Plant downsizing, Aug. FY25 in the Crackers row, the Business integration line, and at about x 54 percent, y 20 percent next to Competitors, etc. Thin black bracket outlines frame the text Becoming a strong business entity able to support domestic industry. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF. Title is black #000000 in MeiryoUI-Bold sans-serif bold, about 3.7 percent of slide height. Subtitle is yellow #FFFF00 bold on slate band #44546A. Body text in the left panel is bold sans-serif at about 2.6 percent of slide height: red #CF5C5C for restructuring items, blue #6C84B2 for PP and MDI items, dark grey #3B3838 for row labels, white on the red #CF5C5C Business integration bar. Accent colours are olive green #ABB867 for borders and the header bar, blue #5B9BD5 for the arrow, and dark red #C00000 for the right-hand panel text and dotted border. Shapes have rounded corners of about 1 to 2 percent of the slide width, the left panel and green boxes have solid borders about 0.4 percent of slide width, the Competitors box has a grey #7F7F7F border, and the right panel has a red dotted border. No shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

A light grey full-slide field with a dark blue section tab labelled Contents at the top left, a large white hexagon with a soft shadow centred on the slide carrying two text groups, and the Mitsui Chemicals logo with a date bar at the top right. The contents text is left-aligned inside the hexagon at about x 22% with right-aligned speaker attributions; there is no footer or slide number visible.

**Visuals**

A large white-to-pale-blue gradient hexagon with a thin white outline and a soft grey drop shadow, pointed left and right, spanning about 8% to 88% of slide width and nearly the full height. Two small triangular play-arrow markers: a grey right-pointing triangle beside the first item and a black right-pointing triangle beside the second item, both about 5% of slide width. The Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right: a blue swoosh mark followed by the dark grey wordmark 'Mitsui Chemicals'. A dark grey date bar beneath the logo reads 'Sep. 10, 2025' in white. The hexagon gradient runs from white at upper left to pale steel blue (#D0D9E2) at lower right.

**Design**

Background light grey #EBEDF1 with a white layer behind. Section tab fill #072884 with white bold title. Grey inactive contents text #AFABAB, active black text #000000. Typefaces are a Meiryo-style humanist sans-serif in bold. Title 'Contents' about 4.45% of slide height; body text about 3.7% for the first group and about 3.3% for sub-bullets; attributions about 3.0%. Hexagon has a white outline about 1% of height and a soft grey shadow; the tab has a pointed right edge with square corners.

### Slide 7

**Text**

7 / Integration of Polyolefins business / ①Polyolefin’s contribution to the domestic industry / Mitsui Chemicals / Sep. 10, 2025 / Polyolefins are made from naphtha produced by petroleum refining and their applications are extend / across various industrial sectors. They are highly important materials from the perspective of economic / security and essential products for domestic industry. / ※source：Based on data from The Japan Plastics Industry Federation and statistics from Japan Petrochemical Industry Association, certain data have been partially estimated in-house. / 1,800 / 1,600 / 1,400 / 1,200 / 1,000 / 800 / 600 / 400 / 200 / 0 / (million tons) / Gross domestic / production of / plastics / 8.4 / 46% / PP / 1.93 / PE / 1.94 / propylene / 2.99 / ethylene / 4.99 / naphtha / Petroleum refining / Cracker / Plastics / Applications of Polyolefins / (million tons) / Films / 1.00 / Laminates / 0.22 / Flat yarn / 0.03 / Injection molding / 1.23 / Blow molding / 0.23 / Fiber / 0.08 / Pipes / 0.06 / Extrusion molding / 0.19 / Electric wire sheathing / 0.04 / Others / 0.53 / ※source：Japan Petrochemical Industry Association / Relevant Industry / Automotive / instrument panel, bumper etc. / Electric Materials / materials for Lithium-ion battery, control units etc. / Medical Instruments / syringe, dialyzer, infusion bag etc. / Cosmetics・Foods / various containers / Agriculture / films, irrigation pipes / Construction / electric wire sheathing, various pipes / ※The data of 2024 excluding propylene from Olefin Conversion Unit and Fluid Catalytic Cracking

**Layout**

Single 16:9 slide with a navy numbered tab and title row across the top, a full-width olive banner below it, and a three-part body: a left column with two photos and a naphtha arrow, a centre stacked-bar chart with the Cracker and Plastics columns, and a right side with an applications table and a Relevant Industry photo grid of two columns of six rows. Title is left-aligned at about 7.7% from the left edge; the date sits in a grey box at top right under the logo; footnotes sit bottom left and under the table; there is no slide-number footer beyond the navy '7' tab.

**Visuals**

Stacked bar chart titled with unit '(million tons)', with a vertical axis from 0 to 1,800 in steps of 200 (labels 0, 200, 400, 600, 800, 1,000, 1,200, 1,400, 1,600, 1,800 at the left, grey #595959 bold) and horizontal gridlines in light grey. Two columns: Cracker, with segments ethylene 4.99 (light blue #CAEEFB, bottom, red outline), propylene 2.99 (pink #F2CFEE, red outline), then unlabelled segments in grey #D9D9D9, salmon #F6C6AD and light green #D9F2D0 up to about 1,650 (no printed values, about); Plastics, with PE 1.94 (light blue #CAEEFB, bottom), PP 1.93 (pink #F2CFEE), and unlabelled grey segments above (about) up to the 8.4 total labelled in a red-outlined callout box reading Gross domestic production of plastics 8.4. A red-outlined bracket beside the Plastics column points to '46%' in bold red #FF0000 inside a dark grey outlined box. Thin blue connector lines run from the Plastics column top to the applications table and from its base to the table footnote. Right of the chart is a table 'Applications of Polyolefins' with header '(million tons)' and rows: Films 1.00; Laminates 0.22; Flat yarn 0.03; Injection molding 1.23; Blow molding 0.23; Fiber 0.08; Pipes 0.06; Extrusion molding 0.19; Electric wire sheathing 0.04; Others 0.53. Photos: a petrochemical plant with distillation columns and pipes on white background (left top, full colour, boxed); a dusk aerial of a refinery with storage tanks glowing under orange-to-blue sky (left, boxed); an inset of tall columns and pipework over a grey sky (on the chart, boxed); a grid of 12 photographs in two columns: automotive interior dashboard, silver sports car, reel of film and electronic components, Li-ion battery pack with orange cables, syringes on blue, medical dialysis equipment, cosmetic tubes and bottles, brown pouch with coffee beans, green crop row with pipe, blue sky over greenhouse, power line insulators on blue sky, blue PVC pipes. Logo: Mitsui Chemicals (blue swoosh and grey wordmark) at top right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Title 'Integration of Polyolefins business' in bold black #000000 sans-serif (Meiryo UI Bold), about 3.7% of slide height. Banner text white #FFFFFF bold sans-serif about 3.2% of height. Body and table text black #000000 regular sans-serif about 2.2 to 3.0%. Accent colours: olive banner #ABB867, navy tab #072884, red #FF0000 for 46% and chart outlines, grey #595959 for axis labels and date box. Chart segments use pastel fills (#CAEEFB, #F2CFEE, #F6C6AD, #D9F2D0, greys). Header rule thin grey line. Sharp corners, no shadows, no gradients.

**Layout**

Full-width header band across the top with a navy numbered tab at the far left holding the page number 8, the title Integration of Polyolefins business beside it, a two-line subtitle at centre-right, the Mitsui Chemicals logo and a dark grey date box at top right, separated from the body by a thin grey rule. Below, a green banner spans the full width with three lines of centred bold white text. The body is three columns: a stacked area chart at left (about x 1 to 30), a 1994 company list at centre (x 35 to 62) with column heading As of September 1994, a grey arrow in the middle (x 64 to 70) labelled reorganization and consolidation, and a 2025 company list at right (x 71 to 99) with heading As of April 2025. Totals sit under each list and source lines sit at the bottom of the left and centre columns; margins are about 1 to 2 percent of the width; there is no slide number other than the tab at top left.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked area chart titled Trend of domestic production of PP / PE, unit (million tons). Y axis from 0 to 6 in steps of 1 with light grey horizontal gridlines at each step. X axis is calendar years 1979 to 2023 labelled 97, 99, 01, 03, 05, 07, 09, 11, 13, 15, 17, 19, 21, 23 (no printed values for individual years). Three stacked series, bottom to top: High density PE in light sky blue #96DCF8, from 0 to about 1.3 in 1997 and about 0.66 in 2024; Low density PE (including EVA) in pale blue #CAEEFB, from the top of the high density band to about 3.1 in 1997 (total 2.05 above high density) and about 1.28 above the high density band in 2024; PP in pale pink #F2CFEE, the top band, about 2.85 in 1997 (total for the year 2.85, 100%) and 1.93 in 2024 (70%). Printed labels: 1997 2.85 (100%) in black at upper left; PP label in black mid chart; 2.05 (〃) in blue #32ACE7 over low density PE; 1.31 (〃) in blue #0000FF over high density PE; 2024 1.93 (70%) in magenta #FF66FF at right; 1.28 (64%) in blue #32ACE7 at right; 0.66 (50%) in blue #0000FF at right. Black double-headed vertical arrows mark each band at the 1997 and 2024 positions. Legend: none; the bands are labelled directly. Highlighted points: the 1997 and 2024 columns are marked by the arrows. Grey arrow: a solid grey right-pointing block arrow #BBBCBC between the two lists, with a black bold label reorganization above and consolidation below. Logo: Mitsui Chemicals blue swoosh emblem with dark grey wordmark at top right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles are black bold sans-serif (MeiryoUI-Bold style), page title about 3.7 percent of slide height, subtitle and body about 2.2 percent, footers about 2.0 percent. Body text is black #000000 MeiryoUI regular for company names and bold for totals. Banner text is white bold. Accent colours: navy #072884 tab, green banner #ABB867, grey arrow #BBBCBC, chart greys #595959 and #7F7F7F for axis labels, blue #32ACE7, #0000FF, magenta #FF66FF for values. Lines are thin dark grey rules under the column headings. Shapes are square-cornered, with no shadows or gradients. The navy tab has a chevron point to its right.

### Slide 9

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Side-by-side bar comparisons of capacity, demand and 2050 demand make the excess-capacity argument readable without extra text.
- Review: The charts on slides 9, 10 and 14 are readable, though slide 9 repeats the same chart three times with small labels clipped inside bars, and slide 14's waterfall has no running totals or connectors, so the core can be recovered but needs substantial revision.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent header with slide number, the green takeaway banner on every content slide, and the simple three-bar comparison on slide 9.
- Suggested repair: Rebuild slides 5, 12, 13 and 15 so each carries one message with larger type and a simpler diagram or table, and fix the clipped labels in the slide 9 bars and the waterfall on slide 14.

**Layout**

Three equal-width column charts sit side by side under a full-width green banner, each with a bold chart title and thin underline at the top, a unit label at right, a red rounded excess-capacity box, bar columns on a shared baseline and category labels below. The header runs across the top with a dark blue numbered tab at the far left, the title left-aligned beside it, a subtitle at centre-right, the logo and date box at top right; the footnote is bottom right and there is no footer bar.

**Visuals**

Three vertical stacked column charts, each titled with the product and 2024 (PP, LLDPE, HDPE), unit '(million tons)'. Each chart has three columns on a baseline with no visible y-axis or gridlines apart from faint horizontal lines inside the dark bars. Columns per chart: Domestic capacity (solid dark navy #002060 bar, no internal label, total printed above), Domestic demand (mid blue #0070C0 lower segment labelled domestic, with a dashed-outline box above labelled import, total printed above), Domestic demand in 2050 (same stacking, total printed above). PP: capacity 2.62; demand total 2.35 = domestic 1.93 + import 0.41; 2050 total 1.8 = domestic 1.5 + import 0.3. LLDPE: capacity 0.94; demand total 0.93 = domestic 0.58 + import 0.34; 2050 total 0.7 = domestic 0.45 + import 0.25. HDPE: capacity 1.08; demand total 0.8 = domestic 0.59 + import 0.21; 2050 total 0.6 = domestic 0.45 + import 0.15. Each chart has a red rounded-rectangle box titled 'Excess capacity assuming continued 2024 production levels' with two unlabelled values: PP 0.69 and 1.12; LLDPE 0.36 and 0.49; HDPE 0.49 and 0.63. No legend. No highlighted points. Japanese labels (国内能力, 国内需要, 50年需要) are set beneath the English category labels and are not visible.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Green banner #ABB867 with bold white text. Dark navy #002060 for capacity bars, mid blue #0070C0 for domestic segments, dashed dark grey outlines for import boxes, red #E02020 rounded outline for excess capacity boxes. Titles and labels in near-black #0D0D0D to #000000. Typeface MeiryoUI-Bold for header and banner, MeiryoUI regular for chart text; humanist sans-serif. Header title approx 3.7% of slide height bold; banner text approx 3.3%; chart titles about 2.6%; chart labels about 2.0%. Thin grey underline below each chart title. Square-cornered bars, no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 10

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A long earnings time series is annotated with dated plant closures, linking the numbers directly to the events behind them.
- Review: The charts on slides 9, 10 and 14 are readable, though slide 9 repeats the same chart three times with small labels clipped inside bars, and slide 14's waterfall has no running totals or connectors, so the core can be recovered but needs substantial revision.

**Text**

10 / Integration of Polyolefins business / ④Trend of our polyolefins business earnings / Mitsui Chemicals / Sep. 10, 2025 / ・Although Prime Polymer had optimized production and maintained profitability following the 2008 financial / crisis, it has experienced sluggish earnings since 2020 due to the impact of COVID-19 pandemic. / ・Since 2023, it’s earnings have improved driven by production optimization and price adjustments. Looking / ahead we plan to expand earnings by optimizing production capacity through build-and-scrap initiatives. / April 2005 / Establishment of / Prime Polymer / Further profitability improvement initiatives / under standalone operation / (Completing the build-and-scrap and pursuing / further optimization) / Prime / Polymer’s / standalone / sales volume / PP＋PE / （approximate / figure / million / tons） / 2025:High-performance PP plant at Chiba starts up / 2026:1 PP line at Chiba to close / Pursue further optimization / ・March 2011:Ube-PP plant(90K tons) closed / ・March 2013:Chiba-HD plant(130K tons) closed / ・June 2013 :Chiba-2PP plant(100K tons) closed / ・December 2014:Chiba-LLDPE plant(60K tons) closed / ・February 2022:1 PP line at Chiba(100K tons) closed / ・2023-2025：Price adjustment in response to / rising utility and fixed costs / Prime / Polymer’s / standalone / operating / profit / (one hundred / million yen) / 2005 2006 2007 2008 2009 2010 2011 2012 2013 2014 2015 2016 2017 2018 2019 2020 2021 2022 2023 2024 / Plan / 600 / 500 / 400 / 300 / 200 / 100 / 0 / -100 / -200 / 180 / 130 / 80 / 30 / -20 / -70

**Layout**

Single-column slide with a navy numbered tab at the top left (page number 10), the title at top left beside a subtitle, and the Mitsui Chemicals logo with a grey date box at top right. A full-width olive green band sits beneath the header and holds the white bold summary text. Below the band, a line chart sits above a bar chart, both spanning the width between about 12% and 96% of slide width, with annotation text blocks placed over the empty plot area and the axis labels at left and right; the year axis runs along the bottom, with the word Plan at far right. There is no footer or slide number other than the navy tab.

**Visuals**

Line chart (upper): black line of about 2.5% slide-height stroke, plotted from the left axis of sales volume PP＋PE; it starts at about 2005 at a high level, drops to a plateau, has a step down near 2008, bumps up near 2009, falls steadily through 2012 to 2013, dips slightly around 2015, is flat 2016 to 2018, declines through 2019 to 2023, then a dotted extension runs to the right edge of the plot after 2024. Values are printed only as axis ticks 600, 500, 400, 300, 200, 100, 0 on the left and 180, 130, 80, 30, -20, -70 on the right, so no data labels exist; series values are about and not printed. Grey downward triangle marker (about #AAAAAA) above the 2005 start point marks the April 2005 establishment of Prime Polymer. Bar chart (lower): vertical bars in blue #0070C0, one per year 2005 to 2024 with the Plan column at far right, with a thin black horizontal zero baseline. Values are not printed; estimates in units of one hundred million yen read from left axis ticks: 2005 about -12, 2006 about 139, 2007 about 36, 2008 about -160, 2009 about -79, 2010 about 7, 2011 about 32, 2012 about -29, 2013 about 54, 2014 about 79, 2015 about 151, 2016 about 129, 2017 about 132, 2018 about 132, 2019 about 82, 2020 about 35, 2021 about 60, 2022 about 33, 2023 about -18, 2024 about 78, Plan about 165 (no bar in the gap between 2024 and Plan). Y-axis: left ticks 600 to -200 for the line chart and 200 to -200 for bars; the scale is printed only as ticks. Highlighted points: none. Legend: none. Annotations: four bullet lines of plant closures and one bullet line of February 2022 closure and 2023-2025 price adjustment are placed over the plot area; the 2025 and 2026 notes and Further profitability improvement text sit in the upper right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background with a navy #072884 numbered tab and a white chevron notch at top left. Title text is black #000000 in bold Meiryo UI sans-serif at about 3.7% of slide height; subtitle is black bold Meiryo UI about 2.6%. Summary band text is white #FFFFFF bold Meiryo UI about 2.96% of slide height on olive green #ABB867. Chart and annotation text is black #000000 regular Meiryo UI about 2.2% (axis labels 1.8 to 2.2%). Accents: bars #0070C0 blue, navy #072884 tab, grey #595959 date box with white text. Shapes are flat with no corner radius, shadow or gradient; the chart line is black straight segments.

### Slide 11

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 3, 5, 11, 12, 13 and 15 are dense text, tables and tiny diagrams that cannot be read in a briefing, and slide 12's synergy table and slide 15's table are close to unreadable.

**Text**

11
Integration of Polyolefins business
⑤Outline of the integration
Sep. 10, 2025
Integrated domestic polyolefins business of SUMITOMO CHEMICAL COMPANY, LIMITED (“SUMITOMO”) into Prime Polymer Co., Ltd.(“PRM”). SUMITOMO will acquire a stake in PRM as a shareholder.
Outline of PRM
Company name
Prime Polymer Co., Ltd.
Domestic sites
Head office(Tokyo), Sales office(Nagoya, Osaka) Fundamental Research Laboratory, Packaging and Industrial Materials Laboratory, Automotive Materials Laboratory, Ichihara Works, Anesaki Works, Osaka Works,
Affiliated Companies
Evolue Japan Co., ltd.(“Evolue Japan”) Prime Evolue Singapore Pte. Ltd. Tokuyama Polypropylene Co., Ltd.(“Tokuyama PP”)
Established
April 1, 2005
President and Vice president
YOSIZUMI Fumio, President YAMADA Eiichiro, Executive Vice President
Capital
20 billions of yen(Mitsui Chemicals(“Mitsui”) 65% ; Idemitsu Kosan(“Idemitsu”) 35%)
Sales Revenue
286.3 billions of yen（Standalone PRM, FY 24）
Production Capacity
PP:1.26 million tons/year（including Tokuyama PP ） PE:0.55 million tons/year（including Evolue Japan）
Number of Employees
710（as of the end of March 2025）
SUMITOMO’s business to be integrated
Business
・Domestic PP business(including PP-CPD business) ・Domestic LLDPE business(including Evolue Japan) ※excluding domestic LDPE business, overseas affiliated companies*
Sites
Head office(Tokyo), Branch office(Nagoya) Chiba Works, EGM laboratory
Sales revenue
101 billions of yen（FY 24）
Production Capacity
PP :0.33 million tons/year PE :0.17 million tons/year
Number of Employees
Approximately 200
*・PO business in Singapore and Saudi Arabia
・PP-CPD business in Thailand, Europe and the US
・Integrated SUMITOMO’s business listed above into PRM
・SUMITOMO will acquire PRM’s shares for consideration
【Investment ratio in PRM】
・Before integration : Mitsui 65%, Idemitsu 35%
・After integration : Mitsui 52%, Idemitsu 28%, SUMITOMO 20%

**Layout**

Two columns below a full-width green headline band: the left column holds the Outline of PRM table (x about 1 to 52%), the right column holds the SUMITOMO’s business table and notes (x about 55 to 99%). The title sits top-left beside a navy numbered tab, with the subtitle to its right and the Mitsui Chemicals logo and date box in the top-right corner. Text is left-aligned in the table cells, section headings are centred over each column, and a grey arrow connects the right column to the lower-right text.

**Visuals**

Two tables and one block arrow. The left table, Outline of PRM, has two columns (a label column with #F4F4EA fill and a value column with white fill) and 10 rows: Company name; Domestic sites; Affiliated Companies; Established; President and Vice president; Capital; Sales Revenue; Production Capacity; Number of Employees. The right table, SUMITOMO’s business to be integrated, has two columns and 5 rows: Business; Sites; Sales revenue; Production Capacity; Number of Employees, with values as listed in the text. The large grey block arrow (#BBBCBC) points left from the bottom of the right table toward the left table, with a downward stem beneath the table, and has no labels. Logo: Mitsui Chemicals (blue-grey swoosh mark with wordmark) in the top-right. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background is plain white. Titles are black #000000 in a bold sans-serif (Meiryo UI Bold, close to Helvetica Bold); the headline band uses white bold text on olive green #ABB867. Body text in the tables is black #000000 in Meiryo UI regular, about 2.2 to 2.4 percent of slide height. Section headings are dark #0D0D0D in Meiryo UI regular at about 3 percent of slide height. Accent colours: navy tab #072884 with white numeral, dark grey date box, grey arrow #BBBCBC. Table borders are thin black hairlines; label cells use pale beige #F4F4EA. Corners are square with no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 12

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 3, 5, 11, 12, 13 and 15 are dense text, tables and tiny diagrams that cannot be read in a briefing, and slide 12's synergy table and slide 15's table are close to unreadable.
- Suggested repair: Rebuild slides 5, 12, 13 and 15 so each carries one message with larger type and a simpler diagram or table, and fix the clipped labels in the slide 9 bars and the waterfall on slide 14.

**Text**

12
Integration of Polyolefins business
⑥Integration synergy
Sep. 10, 2025
We will realize profitability improvement and increased capital efficiency in polyolefins business by generating integration synergies worth more than 8 billion yen through initiatives including consolidation of facilities and product lines. (Post-integration, we aim to improve ROIC in our polyolefins business by 1-2% compared to the current level.)
Cost reduction effects through rationalization and related initiatives
（billions of yen）

・Additional actions to close and consolidate facilities

about 2

about 8

exceeding 8 billion yen by further initiatives.

initiatives independently by PRM
(Completing of build-and-scrap of PP,

starts up

**Layout**

A white slide with a navy numbered tab in the top-left corner (number 12) and the title 'Integration of Polyolefins business' beside it, with a small subtitle at top centre-left and the Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right above a dark grey date box. A full-width olive green banner holds the white headline statement across the upper third. Below, the left column carries a bold section heading over a black-headed two-column table, with a boxed closing statement beneath it; the right column carries annotated notes and a column chart of 2016 to 2024 plus a future-vision bar, with year labels along the baseline. Margins are about 1% at left and right; there is no footer and no slide-number element other than the tab.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical column chart with no chart title and no printed y-axis values, ranges or units. Categories along the baseline: 2016, 2017, 2018, 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024, Future vision. Series are blue #0070C0 columns, all positive except 2023 which is a small negative bar below the baseline. Estimated heights (about, no values printed): 2016 about 8.4 units, 2017 about 8.5, 2018 about 8.6, 2019 about 5.5, 2020 about 2.0, 2021 about 3.2, 2022 about 2.0, 2023 about -0.8 (below baseline), 2024 about 4.6. The Future vision column is a stacked column: a blue #0070C0 base up to about 11.4 units from baseline and a light blue #B4C7E7 top segment with a black outline (about 8.1 units) labelled 'SUMITOMO’s earnings ＋Integration synergy' with a thin leader line from the label to the segment. Annotations text on the chart: 'February 2022:1 PP line at Chiba(100K tons) closed' and '2023～2025：Price adjustment in response to rising utility and fixed costs'. No legend and no highlighted points beyond the light blue segment. Table: two columns, 'Items' (black header, white bold text) and 'Intended effects' (black header, white bold text); rows: 'Optimization of production capacity' with sub-bullet '・Additional actions to close and consolidate facilities' and '⇒ PP : 1 line, PE : 1 line' | 'about 4'; 'Consolidation of product lines / productivity improvement' | 'about 2'; 'Reduction of administration functions etc.' | 'about 2'; 'Amount of synergies' (right-aligned in the first column) | 'about 8'. Row fills alternate #D9D9D9 and #F2F2F2 in the body; values right-aligned. Logo: Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right, a blue and dark blue swoosh mark with the wordmark 'Mitsui Chemicals' in dark grey. Icons: none. Photographs: none. Other: the olive banner, a white rectangle box with a black outline containing the closing statement.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF. Headline banner olive green #ABB867 with white bold headline text #FFFFFF. Title and slide-number text black #000000 (number white #FFFFFF on navy #072884 tab). Section heading and body text near-black #0D0D0D or black #000000. Accent colours: dark green #046A38 for the highlighted synergy bullets, blue #0070C0 for chart bars, light blue #B4C7E7 for the synergy segment. Typefaces: Meiryo UI, a humanist sans-serif, bold for titles, headline, headings and highlighted bullets, regular for body and labels. Title size about 3.7% of slide height; banner headline about 3.3%; body and table text about 2.6%; chart and note labels about 1.8% to 2.2%. Line styles: thin grey header divider, black table header, grey rule under section heading, black thin axis lines, black bracket lines beside annotations. Corner radii: none (square corners). Shadows: none. Gradients: none. Chevron shapes in the header tab are light grey #C8CCD4.

### Slide 13

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 3, 5, 11, 12, 13 and 15 are dense text, tables and tiny diagrams that cannot be read in a briefing, and slide 12's synergy table and slide 15's table are close to unreadable.
- Suggested repair: Rebuild slides 5, 12, 13 and 15 so each carries one message with larger type and a simpler diagram or table, and fix the clipped labels in the slide 9 bars and the waterfall on slide 14.

**Text**

13 / Integration of Polyolefins business / ⑦Accelerating green transformation by / business integration / Mitsui Chemicals / Sep. 10, 2025 / The renewed PRM aims to become a true prime solution partner for customers in the emerging green society by combining 'Green raw material supply capability by the three investing companies', 'Technologies and channels of PRM（before integration）', 'Technologies and channels from SUMITOMO'. / Sales of products derived from green raw materials / Customers, Consumers / Three investing companies / Supply green raw materials / Renewed PRM / Production・ distribution・ consumption・ disposal / Idemitsu / Waste plastic pyrolysis facility →chemical recycled naphtha / SAF manufacturing facility →bio-based naphtha / Crackers （Product raw materials） / PP・PE plants （Product PO resins） / Sales of high-value-added recycled products / Capture / Capture（chemical recycling） / Products derived from green raw materials （Biomass, Chemical recycling） / PRM（before integration） / SUMITOMO / Enhance our ability to provide solutions through diversification of raw material lineup / High-value-added recycled products （Mechanical recycling） / PRM（before integration） / SUMITOMO / Enhance our ability to develop technologies and markets through proprietary technologies and channel integration / Mitsui, SUMITOMO / ・imported bio-based naphtha / ・chemical recycled naphtha

**Layout**

Header band across the top with a navy numbered tab '13' at the far left, the title 'Integration of Polyolefins business' beside it, a two-line section label in the centre-right, the Mitsui Chemicals logo and a grey date box at top right. A full-width olive banner sits below the header carrying a three-line centred statement. The main area is a left-to-right process flow with a large return arrow sweeping under it, and two boxed comparison panels along the bottom with a dashed vertical divider separating a ship photo and caption at bottom left. No footer or footnote apart from the date; no visible slide-number footer beyond the tab.

**Visuals**

Process diagram with arrows: (1) small pale green block arrow from an industrial icon-like illustration of Idemitsu facility to a photo of crackers; (2) pale green block arrow labelled 'Supply green raw materials' from crackers photo to photo of PP・PE plants; (3) pale green block arrow labelled 'Sales of products derived from green raw materials' from the plants area to the ellipse 'Customers, Consumers'; (4) bright cyan (#00FFFF) block arrow labelled 'Sales of high-value-added recycled products' from plants to customers; (5) cyan curved arrow labelled 'Capture' running from customers back toward the plants, with darker teal (#00CDCD) shading; (6) large sage-green (#9EB491) curved return arrow labelled 'Capture（chemical recycling）' running from the customers ellipse area back under the diagram to the Idemitsu side with a pale green arrowhead. Photo 1: crackers, a refinery-type plant with tall grey columns, pipework and a red-and-white striped stack, under bright overcast sky, full-colour, rectangular, not cropped. Photo 2: PP・PE plants, an aerial-style photo of a petrochemical plant with towers, pipes and a road and river at the bottom, bright daylight, rectangular box, not cropped. Illustration: small grey and orange industrial plant icon with tanks, stacks and pipes at left above the Idemitsu label. Photo 3: a tanker ship in blue water with red hull stripe, aerial top-down view, rectangular, bottom-left. Two comparison panels: left panel with pale green header (#C5E0B4) and dark green border, with logos Prasus (green swirl mark and black wordmark) × Meguri (teal swoosh and black wordmark 'CIRCULARITY FOR ALL') and a pink (#FF99FF-like) large X between them; right panel with cyan header (#00FFFF) and navy border, with logos Re'PRM (red and orange swirl and black wordmark) × Meguri (teal swoosh, 'CIRCULARITY FOR ALL') and a pink X between them. Logos: Mitsui Chemicals (blue swoosh mark and grey wordmark) top right. No charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Plain white #FFFFFF background. White background. Header area white with a thin grey rule beneath it. Navy #072884 numbered tab with white bold number. Titles in black #000000 bold sans-serif (MeiryoUI-Bold style, Meiryo UI Bold sans-serif). Main title about 3.7% of slide height; section label about 2.6%; banner text about 2.96% in white bold. Body and labels black #000000 bold sans-serif at about 2.2% to 2.6%. Accent colours: dark green #385723 for green-themed labels and captions; navy #003366 for high-value labels and captions. Olive banner #ABB867 with white bold centred text. Date box dark grey #595959 with white text. Shapes have dark green #385723 outlines around block arrows and green panels, navy outlines around cyan panel. Panel headers filled. Block arrows have a slight shadow on text labels. Ellipse filled pale yellow #FFE699 with black text. Dashed thin grey divider line. No gradients except soft shading on the return arrow and teal arrow.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A waterfall-style bridge breaks FY24 operating income into plant failure, initiatives and restructuring costs to reach an underlying figure.
- Review: The charts on slides 9, 10 and 14 are readable, though slide 9 repeats the same chart three times with small labels clipped inside bars, and slide 14's waterfall has no running totals or connectors, so the core can be recovered but needs substantial revision.
- Suggested repair: Rebuild slides 5, 12, 13 and 15 so each carries one message with larger type and a simpler diagram or table, and fix the clipped labels in the slide 9 bars and the waterfall on slide 14.

**Layout**

Header strip across the top with a navy numbered tab at the far left, the slide title in bold black beside it, a two-line subtitle and a grey date box with the Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right, all above a thin grey rule. Below, a full-width olive green band carries the three-line white summary statement. Beneath it two columns: a left chart column (heading box at top, bar chart with labels below) and a right boxed panel with a green outline listing three numbered factors with values in a right-hand column. Margins are about 1 to 2 percent of slide width; there is no footer and no slide number other than the navy tab reading 14.

**Visuals**

Waterfall chart titled 'Profitability of B&GM business' in a white box with olive outline. Y-axis label 'Operating income before special items (billions of yen)', no visible axis scale or numeric tick range. Bars left to right: FY 24 starts at about -11.4 billion (light green #C5E0B4, printed value ▲11.4 above top-left, bar hangs below the baseline, about 16 percent of slide height tall); step ① Elimination of the negative impact of plant failure, about +10.5 (dark blue #2E75B6, labelled ①, floats from about -11.4 to about -0.9); step ② Effects of initiatives to improve profitability, about +13 (amber #FFC000, labelled ②, rises above baseline); step ③ Adding costs with business restructuring, etc., about -2 (pink #FF99FF, labelled ③, small bar just above baseline); final bar Underlying profitability, 10 (light green #C5E0B4, printed value 10 above it, rises above baseline). A thin black horizontal baseline runs across the chart at zero. Circled numbers ①②③ sit under the step bars. Right panel: green outlined box (#00B050) with three numbered factors and printed values +10.5, +13, △2 in bold black, and the unit (billions of yen) at bottom right. Legend: none.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, covering the full slide. White background; navy #072884 numbered tab at top left; titles and headline text in black #000000 except the white headline text on olive band; olive green band #ABB867 with white bold text; body text black #000000; accents olive #ABB867 outlines, green #00B050 panel border, bar colours light green #C5E0B4, blue #2E75B6, amber #FFC000 and pink #FF99FF. Typeface is MeiryoUI bold for headings and MeiryoUI regular for body, a humanist sans-serif. Slide title about 3.5 percent of slide height, headline and box text about 3.3, body text about 2.2 to 2.6, footnote labels about 1.8. Boxes have square corners, 1 to 2 px outlines, no shadows or gradients; the header rule has a faint grey shadow beneath.

### Slide 15

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 3, 5, 11, 12, 13 and 15 are dense text, tables and tiny diagrams that cannot be read in a briefing, and slide 12's synergy table and slide 15's table are close to unreadable.
- Suggested repair: Rebuild slides 5, 12, 13 and 15 so each carries one message with larger type and a simpler diagram or table, and fix the clipped labels in the slide 9 bars and the waterfall on slide 14.

**Layout**

A full-width header band across the top holds the navy slide-number tab at far left, the bold black title to its right, a two-line subtitle at upper right, and the Mitsui Chemicals logo above a dark grey date box at the far right; a thin grey rule separates it from the body. Below, a full-width olive green banner carries the centred white headline, and the body is three columns: a narrow boxed cash-flow panel at left (about 0.5 to 21.5 percent of width), a large green-headed table from about 21.5 to 83.5 percent, and a right-hand text column from about 84 to 99 percent. Outer margins are about 0.5 percent; there is no footer.

**Visuals**

Left panel: a bar-style cash-flow diagram with three vertical bars standing on a black baseline line (about 0.7 to 15.3 percent of width at y 93 percent). A pale blue bar (#B4C7E7) at x 2.7 percent is labelled Operating cash flow, a tall olive green bar (#ABB867) at x 9.7 percent (upper part, y 42.6 to 62.2 percent) is labelled Free cash flow, and a pale yellow bar (#FFE699) at x 9.7 percent (lower part, y 62.2 to 93.3 percent) is labelled Investing cash flow with the note Investments to maintain production and pursue restructuring. A solid olive green block arrow (#ABB867), about x 12.9 to 18.6 percent and y 51 to 58 percent, points right from the top of the green bar, and the words Pursue investments for a green transition sit above it in dark olive. No numeric values are printed on the bars. Table: a grid of thin black cell borders on white with a green header row (#ABB867) across the top, four columns Business, Ideal state, 2025～2030 Promoting the second phase of restructuring and bolstering downstream business, and Improving profitability (billions of yen), and six body rows labelled Crackers, PH, PTA・PET, Polyurethanes, Polyolefins (PP・PE) and an unlabelled integration row. The Improving profitability column shows +17 spanning the first five rows and Integration synergy more than +8 in the last row. Right column: a thin dark slate bracket (#44546A) spans from the top of the table to its bottom and points to the text block; a grey plus sign (#A6A6A6) sits between Current underlying profitability 10 and the text Promoting the second phase...more than+25; a solid grey down arrow (#808080) sits above the final block. Logo: the Mitsui Chemicals logo at top right, a blue (#1F4E9A) swoosh mark to the left of a dark grey bold wordmark Mitsui Chemicals.

**Design**

Background pure white (#FFFFFF). Page-number tab navy #072884 with white bold number. Title and subtitle in black #000000 bold Meiryo UI style sans-serif; the title is about 3.5 percent of slide height, the subtitle about 2.6 percent, the banner headline about 3.3 percent in white bold, body and table text about 1.8 to 2.2 percent regular or bold. Accents: olive green #ABB867 for the banner, table header, panel border and header tab; dark olive #6D7838 for the Pursue investments text; red #FF0000 for most table plan text; blue #3333FF for the MDI and PP lines; black for other body text. Lines are thin black table rules; the panel has a 1 to 2 pixel olive border with rounded corners of about 1 percent radius; the header tab of the panel is rounded at its top corners. No shadows except a faint grey shadow under the header rule; no gradients.
